Let's design hybrid algorithm with several components:
1. Initial candidates:
   - parent height_sequence_1 (normalize, trim zeros, maybe lengths).
   - Several constructed initial density v heuristics: e.g., V-shaped, two/three-interval, sinusoidal, random smooth, and parent resampled to different n.
2. Optimize each candidate with a robust local solver using:
   - FFT score.
   - Alternating fixed-f convex sub-solution using zero-sum game (multiplicative weights/fictitious play) with line search mix.
   - Direct log-space gradient steps with active-set weights from current self-convolution.
   - Adaptive step sizes/simulated annealing restarts.
3. Track best exact score using np.convolve (or direct objective) for top candidates, throttled.
4. Return best scaled.

Let's revisit the previous LP-inspired approach. The previous parent may have been produced by that. We can improve by solving the convex subproblem min max(f*b) more exactly with dual variables. There is an algorithm for zero-sum games with payoffs via multiplicatively weighted averages that has regret O(sqrt(T log m/n)). We can run many iterations until stable. For each fixed f, we can compute b*, λ* for the game. Then the optimal b is maybe the average of b trajectories (fictitious play) or the last smoothed minimizer. The average of minimizer best responses over time approximates optimal primal (regret). We can maintain b_hist average. This might yield a better update than single current b. Let's detail.

Game: row λ (convolution index x), column b (coordinate i), payoff H_{x,i}=f_{x-i}. Column player minimizes expected payoff, row maximizes.
Algorithm:
- Initialize b uniform, λ uniform.
- For iter: compute h = H b (row payoffs). Choose row best response/softmax λ (e.g., top few). Compute g=H^T λ (column payoffs). Choose column best response/softmax b (e.g., inverse softmax). Record b.
- After T, return empirical distribution of λ (maybe) and b (average of b or softmax of average g).
Value v = min max bound. The average b has expected max? By minimax, average column strategy has max payoff ≤ value + regret? For finite games, if column uses uniform mixture of last best responses to λ_t, then for any row λ, expected payoff ≈ average λ^T H b_t ≤ ... Might be good. But we can just use b that minimizes smoothed max with λ history.

Maintain w_b (log b - logsum). w_lam? λ probability over 2n-1 shifts.

- h=conv(f,b) via FFT. (h sum=1)
- update λ: choose softmax(τ h) or topK. Could maintain λ as a running average of best responses? For dicontinuous game, row strategy should approximate distribution over peaks. We can set λ = softmax(γ h) with increasing γ, or top active average. For f, h values peak/sum.
- g=H^T λ (cross-correlation). This is gradient of expected payoff for column: g_i=∑_x λ_x H_{x,i}.
- update b: softmax(w_b + step * g?) Column minimizes λ^T H b, so move mass to small g: w_b += -η g (then normalize). To keep sum? normalize. Or b_i ∝ exp(-η g_i).
This is similar to previous cross_inner_step but without Adam and with λ as softmax h maybe. Pseudo:
```
def local_search(a_start, budget_s, rng, verbose=False):
    start=time.monotonic(); budget=max(0,budget_s)-5
    deadline=start+budget
    f=normalize_nonneg(a_start); n=f.size
    if n<16: ...
    N2=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    N3=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    phase = exp(-2j*pi*arange(N3//2+1)*(n-1)/N3)
    F = rfft(f,N3); FR = phase*conj(F)
    F_N2 = rfft(f,N2)
    A2, c, score = self_fft(f,N2)
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=score
    top=[(score,f.copy())]
    best_exact = f.copy(); best_exact_score=INF
    exact_time=0; exact_calls=0; last_exact=start
    exact_limit=max(5,0.08*budget) maybe
    # validate initial exact if n manageable
    ...
    b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b)
    w_f=log_weights(f)
    beta_cross=8.0; step_cross=0.1
    beta_self=8.0; step_self=0.08
    stall=0; outer=0; mix_t=0.3
    b_avg=f.copy()
    # maybe lambda history length 2n-1? No.
    while time < deadline:
       # safety
       # cross iterations
       for it in range(2 or 4):
          h, hmax = cross_fft(f,b,N2?) # cross length 2n-1; use N2=next_fast_len(2n-1)
          lam = softmax_weights(h, beta_cross) # or top
          g = corr(f,lam,N3,phase,FR)
          # update b in log via multiplicative + Adam? Use simple exponential with clip.
          # We can keep b direction: w_b += step_cross * (-g)
          # But g may have large values; normalize g relative to its max.
          g_scale = max(1.0, max(g))
          dw = -g / g_scale
          # To avoid b too concentrated, maybe subtract mean? Since normalize anyway, constant irrelevant. Just add.
          w_b += step_cross * dw
          b = softmax(w_b)
          # occasionally update b_avg
       # Evaluate best b among current, b_avg maybe. We need track b that minimizes hmax. During inner, if hmax < best_hmax, store b_best, b_avg?
       # After inner, line search mixtures f+(t)b_best
       # For t in candidate list, including previous best mix_t, do candidate=(1-t)f+t*b_best; normalize; score_fft.
       # If improved, f=best; update w_f; update FR etc.
       # If t close1, b=f else keep b.
       # self update every e.g. 2 outer
       # ...
```

Need track b_best: initialize b_best=f, h_best=max(f*f). In cross inner, after computing h for b, if hmax < h_best: h_best=hmax; b_best=b.copy(). Also b_avg = (1-avg_decay)b_avg + decay b? Use only if hmax decent. b_avg may reduce variance. Evaluate mixtures with b_best and b_avg.

But cross inner using b updates may not converge in 2 iters. Need maybe 8-15 iters per outer. With n=5000, each inner: cross FFT N2 (16k) + rfft(f)+?), corr FFT N3 (16k) with P. Each FFT 16k ~0.0005s? Maybe 10 iters ~20ms per outer; 2400s millions? But exact validation dominates? Actually numpy FFT 16k ~0.1ms? With Python overhead maybe 0.5ms. 10 iters 5ms. We can do many. Python loop overhead matters but okay.

Maybe N3=32768 for n8192, FFT slower. Still okay.

Compute F and FR each f update. For cross h, we need B = rfft(b,N2) each inner. For corr, need P = rfft(lam,N3) each inner. This is 3 or 4 FFTs per inner. Could be okay.

We can optimize: For cross h, if we use N3 instead of N2? f*b length 2n-1, N3 also works; then one planar FFT length. We can just use N=N3 for all operations, since N3≥2n-1. Then no separate N2. Score f*f with N3 works. Simpler. For n=8192, N3=32768; f*f could use 16384 but 32768 okay (double time). Maybe acceptable. Use single N=next_fast_len(3n-2) for everything. So in local_search:
- N=next_fast_len(3n-2)
- phase, F, FR.
- Function `self_score(f)` uses `A=rfft(f,N); c=irfft(A*A,N)[:2n-1]`.
- Function `cross_score(b)` uses `B=rfft(b,N); h=irfft(F_cache? But F fixed for f; if f not changed)`. In inner, f fixed, cache F. B=rfft(b,N); h=irfft(F*B,N).
- Function `corr_from_weights(lam)` uses `P=rfft(lam,N); g=irfft(P*FR,N)[n-1:2n-1]`.

Now, update b via multiplicative weights. Need avoid overflow: maintain w_b centered.
```
def log_space_update(w, updates, lr):
   w += lr * updates
   if not finite: reset
   w -= max(w)
```
But if update -g, g can be negative? f nonnegative, λ nonnegative, so g≥0. So -g nonpositive. b_i decrease where g high. Normalize via softmax. Good. If all g equal, no change.

Adaptive step: We can use a learning rate based on g max. Use `lr = step / (max(g)+eps)`, so w update magnitude ~step. In previous Adam, lr absolute. Let's use `dw = -g / gmax; w_b += lr_b * dw`, with lr_b ~0.5-2. If gmax=0, no. To prevent b becoming too spiky, we can regularize: `g_reg = g + eps` or use `g - m*g.mean()`? Since constant invalid in log after normalization? Actually adding constant to all dw cancels after normalization, so only relative matters. g can be very small for some i; b_i may become extremely small. That's okay (zeros allowed). But too spiky may harm. We can clip `g` at lower bound? Use `g_for_update = np.maximum(g, 1e-12)`? That is huge relative; no. Instead use `dw = -(g - g_min?)` or softmax. If some g=0, b stays. The optimal b may have zeros. Good.

Could use softmax re-centering causing underflow? `softmax_weights(w_b)` with max subtract; if w very negative, exp underflow to0. If all underflow? Could detect sum=0 and fallback uniform or previous. Implement stable softmax with if s<=0 return uniform or keep previous.

After f update, we need recompute c and F/FR. We can also if t=1, b=f reset but maybe keep b_avg.

Self update:
- `c` and `score` from f. `lam_self = softmax_weights(c, beta_self)` (or active weights top). `g_self = corr(lam_self)`.
- Update `w_f` with lr_self: `w_f += lr_self * (-g_self / g_self_max)`; `f_new=softmax(w_f)`. Evaluate score. If improves, accept; else revert. Could also do multiple step sizes (0.5,1,2) and accept best. This is like line search in steepest descent. Since w_f is long-term; if reject, keep old w_f. If accept, set w_f.
- We can combine self and cross by using `b` as direction to reduce cross; self update directly reduces f*f via active peaks. It may duplicate.

Adaptive beta: Increase beta slowly to focus on true maxima. But if beta too high, λ one-hot causing sparse gradient and noisy. Keep beta around 5-20. Use top-K active instead of very high beta. We can implement `peak_weights(c, top_frac=0.05, beta=10)`:
  mask = c >= np.quantile(c, 0.95) maybe expensive O(n). Simpler: threshold = max(c)*(1 - top_frac). top_frac=0.05 gives top5% if distribution skewed. For flat, many. Then softmax over unmasked with beta; zero others. This highlights peaks. Could be better than all softmax for truncated conv. Let's implement `make_weights(c, beta, top_eig=0.0)`; top_eig fraction. Now, let's design exact/FFT score normalization:
All f normalized sum=1. FFT score = 2*n*max(c). Exact score_array after clipping and sum S: 2*n*max(conv)/(S*S). If sum=1, same. Due numerical, sum may not exactly1; normalize before.

Now, local_search step details with pseudo-code and parameter choices:
```
def local_search(a_start, extra_starts, budget_s, rng, verbose=True):
    start=time.monotonic()
    budget=max(0,budget_s)-5
    deadline=start+budget
    f=normalize_nonneg(a_start)
    n=f.size
    if n<64: f=...; n=...
    if n>MAX_N: truncate/reshape? Maybe cap 12000.
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    phase=...
    update_F(f)
    A,c,score=...
    best_f, best_score
    top = [(score, f.copy())]
    b = f.copy()
    b_best = f.copy(); b_bound = score # max f*f
    b_avg = f.copy()
    w_f = log_weights(f)
    w_b = log_weights(b)
    beta = 8.0
    top_frac = 0.08
    lr_cross = 0.8
    lr_self = 0.5
    stall=0; outer=0; last_improve=start
    exact_limit=max(10,0.10*budget)
    exact_score_best=INF; exact_time=0; last_exact=start
    validate exact initial.
    # maybe if initial score >1.9, do quick random Fourier perturbation
    while time < deadline:
        outer +=1
        # safety reset if invalid
        # cross phase
        cross_iters = 4 if outer%3==0 else 6? Maybe 8.
        for it in range(cross_iters):
            B = rfft(b,N)
            h = irfft(F*B,N)[:2n-1]
            if any negative: h=np.maximum(h,0)
            hmax = max(h)
            if hmax < b_bound: b_bound=hmax; b_best=b.copy()
            # b_avg updating: use dec = 0.5/(it+1)? or 0.1
            b_avg = 0.9*b_avg + 0.1*b
            lam = make_weights(h, beta, top_frac)
            P = rfft(lam,N)
            g = irfft(P*FR,N)[n-1:2n-1]
            g = where(finite,g,0)
            gm = max(g)
            if gm > 0:
                w_b += lr_cross * (-g / gm)
                w_b = recenter(w_b)
                b = softmax(w_b)
                if not sum >0: b=previous
        # periodic exact validate best_f
        # candidate mixes:
        candidates = [b_best, b_avg] maybe if b_avg finite

        for directive? For each b_cand evaluate mixtures.
        Choose best_cand_score. If < f_score:
            f=best; f_score=...

            w_f=log_weights(f); update F/FR; b = f.copy() if t==1 else keep b maybe reinitialize w_b? If t small, keep b but b's length same; maybe b_avg too.
            stall=0; lr_cross = min(1.5, lr_cross*1.05); lr_self=min(1.2, ...); beta maybe increase
        else:
            stall +=1; lr_cross=max(0.2, lr_cross*0.95)
        # self phase every other or if stall

            if saff < f_score: f_score=saff (FFT refine) maybe if < best update.
            lam_s = make_weights(c2, beta, top_frac_self=0.05)
            g = corr(lam_s)
            # try step sizes
            for lr in (lr_self,0.5*lr_self,2*lr_self):
                w_try = w_f - lr * g / max(g)
                f_try=softmax(w_try)
                sc=fft_score_only(f_try,N)

                else if best_candidate keep? Choose best among tried maybe.
            if accepted: stall=0; lr_self*=1.05; beta*=1.03
            else: stall+=1; lr_self*=0.9; beta=max(3,beta*0.95)
        # periodic relax beta if too high? 
        # restart if stall > 80

            # maybe if best exact not updated too long, try start
            if rng.random()<0.5 or f_score > best_score +0.03:
                 f = best_f.copy(); f_score=best_score
            else:
                 # pick random start
                 if extra_starts: f = normalize(extra_starts[rng.integers(len(extra_starts))])
                 else: f = softmax(log_weights(best_f)+rng.normal(0,0.15,n))
            reset states
            stall=0; beta=8; lr_cross=0.8; lr_self=0.5
        # adjust beta slowly
        if outer%50==0: beta = min(40, beta*1.05+0.1)
        # exact validation throttled: if time since last >30 and exact time < limit: validate best_f; also if best_score improved by >1e-5 since last exact
    # final exact for top candidates
    for top list: exact if time
    return best_exact, best_f, best_score
```

def softmax_from_w(w):
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, -1e300)
    m = float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return uniform
    e = np.exp(w-m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       # find finite positions
       finite = np.isfinite(w)
       if np.any(finite): e=np.zeros_like(w); e[finite]=1; s=finite.sum()
       else: e=np.ones_like(w); s=w.size
    return e/s
```

normalize_nonneg:
```
def normalize_nonneg(v):
    arr = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: arr=np.ones(1)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not finite or s<=0: arr=np.ones(arr.size if arr.size>0 else 1); s=arr.size
    return arr/s
```
Ensure no boolean: input lists from numpy. Good.

log_weights:
```
def log_weights_from_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.where(finite,a,0)
    a=np.maximum(a, EPS)
    w=np.log(a)
    w=np.where(finite,w,0)
    m=np.max(w)
    w -= m
    return w
```
If all zeros? normalize before so not.

softmax:
```
def softmax_from_w(w):
    w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
    if w.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    w=np.where(finite,w, -1e300)
    m=float(np.max(w))
    if not finite(m): return np.ones(w.size)/w.size
    e=np.exp(w-m)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not finite(s) or s<=0:
       finite_mask=np.isfinite(w)
       if np.any(finite_mask):
          e=np.zeros(w.size); e[finite_mask]=1.0; s=finite_mask.sum()
       else: e=np.ones(w.size); s=w.size
    return e/s
```
Receniter:
```
def recenter_w(w):
    w=np.where(finite,w,0)
    m=float(np.max(w))
    if finite(m): w=w-m; else w=0
    return w
```
Maybe in-place not possible due np.where returns new.

make_weights:
```
def make_weights(c, beta, top_frac=0.0):
    c=np.asarray(c,float).ravel()
    m=c.size
    if m==0: return np.ones(1)
    c=np.where(finite,c,0)
    L=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite(L) or L<=0: return np.ones(m)/m
    x=(c-L)/L
    x=np.where(finite(x), x, -1e300)
    y=np.clip(float(beta)*x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(y)
    if top_frac > 0.0:
       thresh = L*(1.0 - float(top_frac))
       mask = c >= thresh
       if int(mask.sum()) < 8:
          # Use top fixed count
          k = min(m, max(8, int(m*top_frac)))
          idx = np.argpartition(-c, min(k, m-1))[:k]
          mask = np.zeros(m,bool); mask[idx]=True
       e = np.where(mask, e, 0.0)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if not finite(s) or s<=0:
       if top_frac>0: idx as top; e=0; e[idx]=1; s=k
       else: e=np.ones(m); s=m
    return e/s
```
Need `np.argpartition` every time might be overhead if top_frac. For n=10000, 2n=20000, every inner; too expensive. Better threshold mask without argpartition; if count<8, just set threshold=0 maybe. Use:
```
if top_frac>0:
  thresh=L*(1-top_frac)
  mask = c >= thresh
  if mask.sum()<4: mask = c >= L*0.5  # fallback
  if mask.sum()<1: mask[0]=True
```
For many flat c, threshold near L? If all equal L, mask all true. Good. If one max, top 5% maybe few; threshold low enough if distribution smooth. Use top_frac=0.1 maybe count ~10%. Good. Avoid argpartition.

`fft_score_only(a,N)`:
```
def fft_score_only(a,N):
    n=a.size
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    if c.size:
       mn=float(np.min(c)); if mn<0: c=np.maximum(c,0)
       mx=float(np.max(c))
    else: mx=0
    if not finite: INF
    return 2.0*n*mx
```
No need return c. For local, need A,c. `fft_self(a,N)`:

A=rfft; c=irfft; clamp; score=2nmx; return A,c,score
```

```

Exact score:
```
def exact_score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return INF
    a=np.where(finite,a,0); a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not finite(s) or s<0.01? Local sums 1. If s<=0 return INF
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    mx=float(np.max(b))
    if not finite(mx): return INF
    val=2.0*n*mx/(s*s)
    return val if finite else INF
```
Matches eval except eval clamps large to1000; our values small.

Resize_array:
```
def resize_array(a, new_n):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    old_n=a.size
    if old_n==new_n: return a.copy()
    if new_n<=0: new_n=16
    if old_n==1: return np.full(new_n, a[0]/new_n?) normalize later
    # use vectorized linear interpolation
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    # For speed, use np.interp
    arr=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_nonneg(arr)
```
For old_n large, np.interp O(n log n?) okay few times.

For load if >MAX_N, `resize_array(arr, 8192)`.

Make fourier candidate:
```
def make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=3, deterministic=False, phase=None):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    angles_base=2*pi*i/n
    v=np.ones(n)
    if K>=1:
       a1 = 1.0 if deterministic else rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
       p1 = phase if phase is not None and deterministic else rng.uniform(0,1)*2*pi
       v += a1*np.cos(angles_base + p1)
    if K>=2:
       a2 = rng.uniform(0,0.35)
       p2 = rng.uniform(0,1)*2*pi
       v += a2*np.cos(2*angles_base + p2)
    if K>=3:
       a3 = rng.uniform(0,0.15)
       p3 = rng.uniform(0,1)*2*pi
       v += a3*np.cos(3*angles_base + p3)
    # Optional tiny random smooth noise? Not in this function.
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    return normalize_nonneg(v)
```
For deterministic sinusoid, use `make_fourier_candidate(n,rng,1,True,phase)`.

Could use phase in cycles (0..1) to avoid p*2pi? Use rad.

Make starts for length:
```
def make_starts_for_length(n, parent, rng, num_random=30, include_parent=True):
   starts=[]
   scores=[]
   if parent is not None:
      a=resize_array(parent,n); starts.append(a)
   # deterministic sinusoids: 8 phases
   for j in range(8):
       phase = 2*pi*j/8

           a=softmax_from_w(w)
           starts.append(a)
   # There may be duplicates; score and sort.
   scored=[]
   for a in starts:
      norm=normalize_nonneg(a)
      sc=fft_score_only(norm, next_fast_len(2*n-1))
      scored.append((sc,norm))

def local_search(a_start, extra_starts, budget_s, rng, verbose=False):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    deadline = start + budget
    f = normalize_nonneg(a_start)
    n = f.size
    if n < 32:
        n0 = f.size
        f = np.ones(1024); f += f? maybe just new; keep? If small, parent? We'll set f = np.ones(4096)/4096
    if n > MAX_N:
        f = resize_array(f, MAX_N)
        n = f.size
    N = next_fast_len(3*n - 2)
    phase = np.exp(-2j*np.pi*np.arange(N//2+1)*(n-1)/float(N))
    F = rfft(f,N); FR=phase*conj(F)
    A2,c2,score = fft_self(f,N)
    best_f=f.copy(); best_score=score
    top_list=add_top_candidate([], f, score)
    b=f.copy(); b_best=f.copy(); b_bound=score; b_avg=f.copy()
    w_f=log_weights(f); w_b=log_weights(b)
    beta=10.0; top_frac=0.10; lr_cross=0.8; lr_self=0.5
    stall=0; outer=0; last_best_time=start
    exact_best=f.copy(); exact_best_score=INF
    exact_time=0.0; exact_calls=0; last_exact=start
    exact_limit=max(10.0,0.10*budget)
    # validate initial
    if exact_limit>0:
       t0=time.monotonic(); sc=exact_score_array(f); exact_time +=...
       exact_calls+=1; last_exact=...
       if sc < exact_best_score: exact_best_score=sc; exact_best=f.copy()
    # If initial high, quick random perturb? maybe not.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       outer +=1
       # safety
       if not finite(score) or not all finite f or not all finite b:
          reset f=best_f; recompute; continue
       # cross
       cross_iters = 6
       if outer % 4 ==0: cross_iters=10
       for it in range(cross_iters):
          B = rfft(b,N)
          h = np.fft.irfft(F*B,N)
          h = h[:2*n-1]
          if h.size:
             if float(np.min(h)) <0: h=np.maximum(h,0)
             hmax=float(np.max(h))
          else: hmax=0
          if finite(hmax) and hmax < b_bound:
             b_bound=hmax; b_best=b.copy()
          # update b_avg
          b_avg = 0.85*b_avg + 0.15*b
          if np.sum(b_avg)<=0: b_avg=b.copy()
          lam = make_weights(h, beta, top_frac)
          P = rfft(lam,N)
          g = irfft(P*FR,N)
          g = g[n-1:2*n-1]
          g = where finite
          gm = max(g)
          if finite(gm) and gm>0:
             w_b = w_b + lr_cross * (-g/gm)
             w_b = recenter_w(w_b)
             b_new = softmax_from_w(w_b)
             if finite and sum>0: b=b_new else revert
       # exact validate if maybe
       if exact_time < exact_limit and (exact_calls==0 or time-last_exact > 30 or best_score < exact_best_score - 1e-4):
           validate best_f
       # line search mixes
       prev_score = score
       best_cand=None; best_cand_score=prev_score; best_t=0.0; best_bid=-1
       t_values=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8,1.0]
       # maybe if previous t in top list add around? We can store last_t variable.
       if last_t !=0: add clip(last_t±0.05)
       b_list = [b_best, b_avg]
       for bid,bc in enumerate(b_list):
           if not all finite bc: continue
           for tval in t_values:
               cand = (1.0-tval)*f + tval*bc
               cs = sum cand; if >0 cand=cand/cs else continue
               sc = fft_score_only(cand,N)
               if finite and sc < best_cand_score:
                   best_cand_score=sc; best_cand=cand.copy(); best_t=tval; best_bid=bid
       if best_cand is not None:
           f=best_cand; score=best_cand_score
           if score < best_score:
              best_score=score; best_f=f.copy(); top_list=add_top_candidate(...); last_best_time=time
           # Accept even if not global? yes f becomes best candidate
           w_f=log_weights(f)
           F=rfft(f,N); FR=phase*conj(F)
           if best_t >= 0.95:
              b=f.copy(); b_best=f.copy(); b_bound=score; b_avg=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b)
           else:
              # keep b but ensure valid; maybe if bid=0 b=b_best else b_avg? But our b current may be from last inner.
              # To be coherent, set b to best_b (bid 0/1) for next phase? We can set b=bc.copy if finite else f.
              if best_bid ==0: b=b_best.copy()
              else: b=b_avg.copy()
              w_b=log_weights(b)
              b_bound = max cross? We can set by evaluating h quickly? Not necessary, set b_bound=INF and b_best=f. Simpler reset b to f? But loses cross progress. Maybe keep b but b_bound=score; b_best=f.
              b_best=f.copy(); b_bound=score
           stall=0; lr_cross=min(1.6, lr_cross*1.05); lr_self=min(1.4, lr_self*1.05); beta=min(40,beta*1.01+0.05)
       else:
           stall +=1; lr_cross=max(0.25, lr_cross*0.97)
       # self step every 2
       if outer % 2 ==0 or stall>8:
          A2,c2,saff=fft_self(f,N)
          if finite(saff):
             if saff < score: score=saff
             if saff < best_score: update best
          if finite(saff):
             lam_s=make_weights(c2, beta, top_frac=0.05)
             P=rfft(lam_s,N); g=irfft(P*FR,N)[n-1:2*n-1]; ...
             gm=max(g)
             if gm>0:
                best_dir=None; best_dir_score=score
                for lr in (lr_self, 0.5*lr_self, 2.0*lr_self):
                    w_try = w_f - lr*(g/gm)
                    w_try = recenter_w(w_try)
                    f_try=softmax_from_w(w_try)
                    sc=fft_score_only(f_try,N)
                    if finite and sc < best_dir_score:
                        best_dir_score=sc; best_dir=f_try; best_w=w_try
                if best_dir is not None:
                    f=best_dir; score=best_dir_score; w_f=best_w
                    F=...; update best if improved; reset b? maybe b=f.copy to avoid bound misc? but cross uses b; could keep but b_best=f. Set b=f? Simpler reset b=f, w_b, b_avg, b_bound=score. But cross subproblem then redo. That's okay after self move.
                    b=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights(b); b_best=f.copy(); b_bound=score; b_avg=f.copy()
                    stall=0; lr_self=min(1.4,lr_self*1.05); beta=min(40,beta*1.01)
                else:
                    stall +=1; lr_self=max(0.2,lr_self*0.95)
       # beta schedule
       if outer % 50 ==0:
          beta = min(50.0, beta*1.05)
          # maybe top_frac increase? top_frac=max(0.03,min(0.2,top_frac*1.1))
       # restart
       if stall > 100 or (time.monotonic()-last_best_time > max(120,0.1*budget)):
          if extra_starts is not None and len(extra_starts)>0 and (rng.random()<0.6 or score > best_score + 0.02):
             idx=int(rng.integers(len(extra_starts)))
             f=normalize_nonneg(extra_starts[idx])
             score=fft_score_only(f,N)
          else:
             sigma = 0.1 + 0.2*rng.random()
             w_try=log_weights_from_sequence(best_f)+rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
             f=softmax_from_w(w_try)
             score=fft_score_only(f,N)
          if not finite(score): f=best_f.copy(); score=best_score
          # If this restart is worse than best by large and not from start? set to best? Maybe leave.
          w_f=log_weights(f); F=...; b=f.copy(); w_b=...
          b_best=f.copy(); b_bound=score; b_avg=f.copy()
          stall=0; beta=10; lr_cross=0.8; lr_self=0.5
          # If score > best_score + 0.2, set back to best? To avoid wasting.
          if score > best_score + 0.2:
             f=best_f.copy(); score=best_score; reset states again
       # exact validate if best exact score not updated and best_score < exact_best_score maybe
    # final exact: validate best_f and top list
    if exact_time < exact_limit*2:
       for sc_top,a_top in top_list (and best_f):
           if time >= deadline - 1: break
           if exact_time > exact_limit*1.8: break
           # skip if top score > exact_best_score + 0.01 maybe
           if finite(exact_best_score) and sc_top > exact_best_score + 2e-3: continue
           sc=exact_score_array(a_top); exact_time += ...
           if sc < exact_best_score: update
    if exact_best_score < INF: return exact_best, exact_best_score, best_f, best_score
    else: return best_f, INF, best_f, best_score
```

def generate_lengths(parent):
    lengths=[]
    if parent is not None:
       p=parent.size
       for val in [p, max(1024,p//2), int(p*1.5), p*2, 2048,4096,8192]:
          if 512<=val<=MAX_N: lengths.append(int(val))
    else: lengths=[2048,4096,8192]
    # filter unique
    seen=set(); out=[]
    for v in lengths:
       if v not in seen: seen.add(v); out.append(v)
    # Sort by distance to parent? We want keep parent first. If parent, ensure p included and maybe first. But sort_keys later by score, not order. For quick time, if many, maybe prioritize parent. We can keep out original order but limit.
    if len(out)>6:
       # if parent, include p, then nearest to p, etc.
       if parent is not None:
          out.sort(key=lambda x: abs(x-parent.size)) # lambda not allowed. Avoid: create tuples (abs, n, idx)
       else sort by n
    return out[:6]

```
def generate_lengths(parent):
  raw=[]
  ... raw unique
  if parent is not None:
     p=parent.size
     dist=[]
     for idx,v in enumerate(raw): dist.append((abs(v-p), v, idx))
  else:
     dist=[]; for idx,v in enumerate(raw): dist.append((v,v,idx))
  dist.sort()
  out=[raw[idx] for ... in dist[:6]]
  return out
```

```
def mix_convolution(c_f, h_fb, c_bb, t, n):
   if t==0: return c_f
   if t==1: return c_bb
   cc = ((1.0-t)**2)*c_f + (2.0*t*(1.0-t))*h_fb + (t*t)*c_bb
   if float(np.min(cc)) <0: cc=np.maximum(cc,0)
   return cc
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try: budget = float(budget_s)
    except: budget=2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <=0: budget=2400
    budget = min(budget, 2395.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    parent = load_initial_sequence()
    # quick selection budget
    quick_budget = min(120.0, max(20.0, 0.05*budget))
    quick_deadline = t0 + quick_budget
    max_len_count = 5 if budget > 1800 else (4 if budget > 800 else 2)
    lengths = generate_lengths(parent)
    if len(lengths) > max_len_count: lengths = lengths[:max_len_count] # already prioritized
    entries=[]
    for n in lengths:
       if time.monotonic() >= quick_deadline: break
       # skip n if <512 or >MAX_N
       if n < 256 or n > MAX_N: continue
       a_quick, sc_fft = quick_best_for_length(n, parent, rng, num_random=10 if n > 8192 else 14)
       a_quick = normalize_nonneg(a_quick)
       # if n changed? no
       sc_exact = INF
       if a_quick.size <= MAX_N:
          t1e = time.monotonic()
          sc_exact = exact_score_array(a_quick)
          # no track, quick approximate
       if not np.isfinite(sc_exact): sc_exact = sc_fft
       entries.append([sc_exact, n, a_quick, sc_fft])
    if not entries:
       n=4096 if parent is None else min(parent.size, MAX_N)
       a=np.ones(n); entries=[[INF,n,a/ n, INF]]
    sort_keys=[]
    for idx,e in enumerate(entries):
       # If exact score is inf, use fft
       s_sort = e[0] if np.isfinite(e[0]) else e[3]
       if not np.isfinite(s_sort): s_sort=10.0
       sort_keys.append((s_sort, e[1], idx))
    sort_keys.sort()
    ordered=[entries[k[2]] for k in sort_keys]
    total_budget = max(0.0, budget - (time.monotonic()-t0) - 10.0)
    if total_budget < 30:
       # return best quick
       best = ordered[0][2] if ordered else np.ones(4096)
       return finalize_sequence(best)
    max_runs = 3 if total_budget > 1500 else (2 if total_budget > 700 else 1)
    runs=[]
    for e in ordered:
       if len(runs) >= max_runs: break
       sc_exact,n,a_quick,sc_fft = e
       # Build starts
       starts=[]
       starts.append(normalize_nonneg(a_quick))
       if parent is not None:
          starts.append(normalize_nonneg(resize_array(parent, n)))
       # deterministic sinusoids at some phases
       for j in range(3):
          starts.append(normalize_nonneg(make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=1, deterministic=True, phase=(2*np.pi*j/3 + rng.uniform(-0.1,0.1)))))
       for _ in range(3):
          K = 1 + int(rng.random()*2)
          starts.append(normalize_nonneg(make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=K)))
       # If parent and n maybe, add noisy resized
       if parent is not None:
          pp=resize_array(parent,n)
          w=log_weights_from_sequence(pp)+rng.normal(0,0.15,n)
          starts.append(softmax_from_w(w))
       # choose best_start by quick score
       best_start=starts[0]
       best_start_score=fft_score_only(best_start, next_fast_len(2*n-1))
       for arr in starts[1:]:
          sc=fft_score_only(arr, next_fast_len(2*n-1))
          if sc < best_start_score:
             best_start=arr; best_start_score=sc
       runs.append([n,best_start,starts,sc_exact,a_quick])
    fractions=[]
    m=len(runs)
    if m==1: fractions=[1.0]
    elif m==2: fractions=[0.65, 0.35]
    else: fractions=[0.50, 0.30, 0.20]
    global_exact_arr = None; global_exact_score=INF
    global_fft_arr = None; global_fft_score=INF
    # Initialize global with quick exact/fft
    for _,_,_,sc_exact,a_quick in runs? Need structure.
```

if not finite(global_fft_score): global_fft_score = global_exact_score if finite else INF
if finite(global_exact_score):
    if finite(global_fft_score) and global_fft_score < global_exact_score - 1e-6 and global_fft_arr is not None:
        return finalize_sequence(global_fft_arr)
    return finalize_sequence(global_exact_arr)
if finite(global_fft_score): return finalize_sequence(global_fft_arr)
# fallback
if parent is not None: return finalize_sequence(parent)
return finalize_sequence(np.ones(4096))
```

def local_search(a_start, extra_starts, budget_s, rng):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    if budget <= 0.0:
       a=normalize_nonneg(a_start)
       sc=exact_score_array(a) #? maybe not
       return a, sc, a, fft_score_only(a,next_fast_len(2*a.size-1))
    deadline = start + budget

    F=np.fft.rfft(f,N)
    FR=phase*np.conjugate(F)
    A2,c_f,score=fft_self(f,N)

    top_list=add_top_candidate([], f, score)

          if h.size:
             if float(np.min(h)) < 0: h = np.maximum(h,0)
             hmax=float(np.max(h))
          else: hmax=0.0

             b_bound=hmax; b_best=b.copy()
          # b_avg slower
          b_avg = 0.9*b_avg + 0.1*b

             s_avg=float(np.sum(b_avg)); if s_avg>0: b_avg=b_avg/s_avg else b_avg=b.copy()
          else: b_avg=b.copy()

          P=np.fft.rfft(lam,N)
          g=np.fft.irfft(P*FR,N)

          g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

          if np.isfinite(gm) and gm>0.0:
             w_b = w_b + lr_cross * (-g/gm)
             w_b = recenter_w(w_b)

          if tval == 0.0: cc=c_f

          else:

def next_fast_len(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    p=1
    while p < m: p <<= 1
    return p

Let's write each.

`make_weights`: Need use `top_frac` float. Implementation:
```
def make_weights(c, beta, top_frac=0.0):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    L = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(L) or L <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    x = (c - L) / L
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -1e300)
    y = np.clip(float(beta) * x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    if top_frac > 0.0:
        thresh = L * (1.0 - float(top_frac))
        mask = c >= thresh
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt < 4:
            thresh = 0.0
            mask = c > thresh
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt < 1:
            mask = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
            int_idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            mask[int_idx] = True
        e = np.where(mask, e, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(m)
    return e / s
```
If top_frac=0.1 and c has values smooth, threshold maybe high but enough. If c max and many small, okay.

`fft_self`:
```
def fft_self(a, N):
    n=a.size
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    if c.size:
       if float(np.min(c)) < 0: c=np.maximum(c,0)
       mx=float(np.max(c))
    else: mx=0
    if not np.isfinite(mx): score=INF
    else: score=2*n*mx
    return A, c, float(score)
```
`grad_from_weights`:
```
def grad_from_weights(lam, FR, N, n):
    P=np.fft.rfft(lam,N)
    g=np.fft.irfft(P*FR,N)
    g=g[n-1:2*n-1]
    g=np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return g
```
`exact_score_array`:
```
def exact_score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return INF
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a,0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01: return INF
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    if b.size==0: return INF
    mx=float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return INF
    val=2.0*n*mx/(s*s)
    if np.isfinite(val): return float(val)
    return INF
```
Note eval returns inf if sum<0.01; our exact same. But our local normalized sum=1.

`add_top_candidate`: Need if score finite. If not, return top.
```
def add_top_candidate(top, a, score, max_items=16):
   if not np.isfinite(score): return top
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if not top:
      top.append((score,a.copy())); return top
   worst_idx=0; worst_score=float(top[0][0])
   for i in range(1,len(top)):
      si=float(top[i][0])
      if si > worst_score: worst_score=si; worst_idx=i
   if len(top) < max_items or score < worst_score:
      if len(top) < max_items: top.append((score,a.copy()))
      else: top[worst_idx]=(score,a.copy())
   return top
```

`resize_array`: ensure int new_n:
```
def resize_array(a,new_n):
   a=normalize_nonneg(a); old_n=a.size
   new_n=int(max(1,new_n))
   if old_n == new_n: return a.copy()
   if old_n == 1:
      val=float(a[0]); return np.full(new_n, val/new_n)
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
   arr=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   return normalize_nonneg(arr)
```
If val=0, full zeros; normalize_nonneg then uniform? Our normalize if sum0 returns ones. Here if val0, returns zeros, then final normalize? Call normalize_nonneg after np.full? We return np.full; in caller maybe normalize. Better:
```
arr=np.full(new_n, val)
return normalize_nonneg(arr)
```
If val0, normalize returns uniform. Good.

`load_initial_sequence`:
```
def load_initial_sequence():
   obj=globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
   if obj is None: return None
   try: arr=np.asarray(obj,float).ravel()
   except Exception: return None
   if arr.size < 16: return None
   arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0)
   arr=np.maximum(arr,0)
   s=float(np.sum(arr))
   if not finite or s<=0: return None
   arr=arr/s
   if arr.size > MAX_N:
      arr=resize_array(arr, 8192 if arr.size>8192 else MAX_N)
   return arr
```
If arr.size > MAX_N resize to 8192, which may drop length. Good.

`make_fourier_candidate`:
```
def make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=2, deterministic=False, phase=0.0):
    n=int(max(1,n))
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    ang=2.0*np.pi*idx/float(n)
    v=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if K >= 1:
        if deterministic:
            a1=1.0; p1=float(phase)
        else:
            a1=0.4 + 1.2*float(rng.random())
            p1=2.0*np.pi*float(rng.random())
        v += a1*np.cos(ang + p1)
    if K >= 2:
        a2=(0.1 + 0.4*float(rng.random())) if not deterministic else 0.25
        p2=2.0*np.pi*float(rng.random()) if not deterministic else 1.1
        v += a2*np.cos(2.0*ang + p2)
    if K >=3:
        a3=0.05 + 0.15*float(rng.random()) if not deterministic else 0.1
        p3=...; v += a3*np.cos(3*ang+p3)
    if K >=4:
       a4=0.03+0.07*...; v += a4*np.cos(4*ang+p4)
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v=np.maximum(v,0.0)
    return normalize_nonneg(v)
```
For deterministic K=1 only no K>=2. If K>1 deterministic, add fixed; not focusing maybe. For deterministic sinusoid call K=1.

def quick_best_for_length(n, parent, rng, num_random=12):
    n=int(n); best_a=None; best_sc=INF; Nq=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    def consider? cannot nested. We'll duplicate:
    if parent is not None:
       a=resize_array(parent,n); a=normalize_nonneg(a)
       sc=fft_score_only(a,Nq)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a
    for j in range(8):
       a=make_fourier_candidate(n,rng,K=1,deterministic=True,phase=2.0*np.pi*j/8.0)
       sc=fft_score_only(a,Nq); update

       a=make_fourier_candidate(n,rng,K=K)
       # maybe also perturb if parent? could add after

       w=log_weights_from_sequence(pp)+rng.normal(0,0.2,n)
       a=softmax_from_w(w); sc update
       w=... + normal 0.5; update
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=np.ones(n,dtype=float)/float(n); best_sc=2.0
    return best_a, float(best_sc)

`generate_lengths`:
```
def generate_lengths(parent):
    raw=[]
    if parent is not None:
       p=parent.size
       vals=[p, max(1024, p//2), int(p*1.5), p*2, 2048, 4096, 8192, 10240]
       for v in vals:
          if v >= 512 and v <= MAX_N: raw.append(int(v))
    else:
       raw=[2048,4096,8192]
    seen=set(); uniq=[]
    for v in raw:
       if v not in seen: seen.add(v); uniq.append(v)
    if parent is not None:
       p=parent.size
       dist=[]
       for i,v in enumerate(uniq): dist.append((abs(v-p), v, i))
    else:
       dist=[]; for i,v in enumerate(uniq): dist.append((v, v, i))
    dist.sort()
    out=[]
    for d,v,i in dist: out.append(uniq[i])
    return out
```
Set. No lambda.

Pseudo code final in actual code:

```
def local_search(a_start, extra_starts, budget_s, rng):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    f = normalize_nonneg(a_start)
    n = int(f.size)
    if n < 512:
        f = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        n = 1024
    if n > MAX_N:
        f = resize_array(f, MAX_N)
        n = int(f.size)
    # If budget <=0 return initial
    if budget <= 1.0:
        sc = fft_score_only(f, next_fast_len(2*n-1))
        exc = exact_score_array(f)
        return f, exc, f, sc
    deadline = start + budget
    N = next_fast_len(3*n - 2)
    phase = ...
    F=...
    FR=...
    A2, c_f, score = fft_self(f,N)
    best_f = f.copy(); best_score = float(score)
    top_list = add_top_candidate([], best_f, best_score)
    b = f.copy(); b_best = f.copy(); b_bound = score/(2.0*n) if finite else 0; b_avg=f.copy()
    w_f = log...; w_b=...
    beta=10.0; top_frac=0.10
    lr_cross=0.8; lr_self=0.5
    stall=0; outer=0; last_best_time=start
    exact_best=f.copy(); exact_best_score=INF
    exact_time=0.0; exact_calls=0; last_exact=start
    exact_limit=max(5.0, 0.10*budget)
    # initial exact
    if exact_limit > 0:
       t0e=time.monotonic(); sc=exact_score_array(f); exact_time +=...
       if finite...
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       outer +=1
       # safety
       if not np.isfinite(score):
          f=best_f.copy(); n=f.size; A2,c_f,score=fft_self(f,N)
          if not finite: f=np.ones(n)/n ... continue
          w_f=...; F=...; b=...
          continue
       # exact validation
       if n <= MAX_N and exact_time < exact_limit:
          need = (exact_calls == 0) or (time.monotonic() - last_exact > 30.0) or (best_score < exact_best_score - 1e-5)
          if need:
             t0e=...
             sc=exact_score_array(best_f)
             ...
       # cross
       cross_iters = 3
       if outer % 5 == 0: cross_iters = 6
       for it in range(cross_iters):
          B = np.fft.rfft(b,N)
          h = np.fft.irfft(F*B,N)
          h = h[:2*n-1]
          if h.size:
             if float(np.min(h)) < 0.0: h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
             hmax=float(np.max(h))
          else: hmax=0.0
          if np.isfinite(hmax) and hmax < b_bound:
             b_bound = hmax; b_best = b.copy()
          b_avg = 0.9*b_avg + 0.1*b
          s_avg=float(np.sum(b_avg))
          if np.isfinite(s_avg) and s_avg > 0.0:
             b_avg = b_avg / s_avg
          else:
             b_avg = b.copy()
          lam = make_weights(h,beta,top_frac)
          g = grad_from_weights(lam, FR, N, n)
          gm = float(np.max(g))
          if np.isfinite(gm) and gm > 0.0:
             w_b = w_b + lr_cross * (-g / gm)
             w_b = recenter_w(w_b)
             b_new = softmax_from_w(w_b)
             s_new = float(np.sum(b_new))
             if np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new > 0.0:
                b = b_new
             else:
                b = best_f.copy()
                w_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)
       # line search with b_best
       b_dir = b_best
       if b_dir.size != n:
          b_dir = f.copy()
       # Ensure finite? We can do finite check with all? Use min? 
       if not bool(np.all(np.isfinite(b_dir))): # all okay rare; but np.all O(n)
          b_dir = f.copy()
       B_dir = np.fft.rfft(b_dir,N)
       h_dir = np.fft.irfft(F*B_dir,N)
       h_dir = h_dir[:2*n-1]
       if float(np.min(h_dir)) < 0.0: h_dir = np.maximum(h_dir,0.0)
       c_dir = np.fft.irfft(B_dir*B_dir,N)
       c_dir = c_dir[:2*n-1]
       if float(np.min(c_dir)) < 0.0: c_dir = np.maximum(c_dir,0.0)
       best_t = 0.0; best_mx = float(score); best_cc = c_f
       for tval in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
          om=1.0-tval
          if tval == 0.0:
             cc = c_f
          elif tval == 1.0:
             cc = c_dir
          else:
             cc = (om*om)*c_f + (2.0*tval*om)*h_dir + (tval*tval)*c_dir
          if float(np.min(cc)) < 0.0:
             cc = np.maximum(cc,0.0)
          mx = float(np.max(cc))
          sc_t = 2.0*n*mx
          if np.isfinite(sc_t) and sc_t < best_mx:
             best_mx = sc_t; best_t = tval; best_cc = cc
       if best_t > 0.0 and best_mx < score - 1e-12:
          f = (1.0-best_t)*f + best_t*b_dir
          sf = float(np.sum(f))
          if np.isfinite(sf) and sf > 0.0:
             f = f / sf
          else:
             f = best_f.copy()
             c_f,score = fft_self(f,N); # reset? 
          if np.isfinite(best_mx):
             score = float(best_mx)
             c_f = best_cc
          else:
             c_f,score = fft_self(f,N)
          if score < best_score:
             best_score=score; best_f=f.copy(); top_list=add_top_candidate(...)
             last_best_time=time.monotonic()
          w_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
          F = np.fft.rfft(f,N)
          FR = phase*np.conjugate(F)
          if best_t >= 0.9:
             b = f.copy(); w_b=log...
             b_best=f.copy(); b_bound=score/(2*n) if finite else 0; b_avg=f.copy()
          else:
             b = b_dir.copy(); w_b=log...
             b_best=b.copy(); b_bound=10.0; b_avg=b.copy()
          stall=0
          lr_cross = min(1.6, lr_cross * 1.05)
          lr_self = min(1.4, lr_self * 1.05)
          beta = min(50.0, beta * 1.01 + 0.05)
       else:
          stall += 1
          lr_cross = max(0.25, lr_cross * 0.97)
       # self
       if outer % 2 == 0 or stall > 8:
          lam_s = make_weights(c_f, beta, 0.05)
          g = grad_from_weights(lam_s, FR, N, n)
          gm = float(np.max(g))
          if np.isfinite(gm) and gm > 0.0:
             best_sc = float(score); best_f_try=None; best_w=None; best_c=None
             for lr in (lr_self, 0.5*lr_self, 2.0*lr_self):
                w_try = w_f - lr * (g / gm)
                w_try = recenter_w(w_try)
                f_try = softmax_from_w(w_try)
                A_try = np.fft.rfft(f_try, N)
                c_try = np.fft.irfft(A_try*A_try, N)
                c_try = c_try[:2*n-1]
                if float(np.min(c_try)) < 0: c_try=np.maximum(c_try,0)
                mx= float(np.max(c_try))
                sc_try = 2.0*n*mx
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc:
                   best_sc=sc_try; best_f_try=f_try; best_w=w_try; best_c=c_try
             if best_f_try is not None:
                f=best_f_try; w_f=best_w; c_f=best_c; score=float(best_sc)
                F = np.fft.rfft(f,N); FR=phase*np.conjugate(F)
                if score < best_score: update
                b=f.copy(); w_b=log...; b_best=f.copy(); b_bound=score/(2*n) if finite else 0; b_avg=f.copy()
                stall=0
                lr_self = min(1.4, lr_self*1.05)
                beta = min(50.0, beta*1.01 + 0.05)
             else:
                stall += 1
                lr_self = max(0.2, lr_self*0.95)
       # beta/top_frac schedule
       if outer % 50 == 0:
          beta = min(50.0, beta*1.05)
          top_frac = max(0.04, min(0.18, top_frac*1.1))
       # restart
       if stall > 120 or (time.monotonic() - last_best_time > max(120.0, 0.2*budget)):
          chosen=None
          if extra_starts is not None:
             for arr in extra_starts:
                if arr.size == n:
                   chosen=arr; break
          if chosen is not None:
             f = normalize_nonneg(chosen)
          else:
             sigma = 0.1 + 0.2*float(rng.random())
             w_try = log_weights_from_sequence(best_f) + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
             f = softmax_from_w(w_try)
          A2,c_f,score = fft_self(f,N)
          if not np.isfinite(score):
             f = best_f.copy(); A2,c_f,score = fft_self(f,N)
          if score < best_score: update? If restart from extra maybe no. But if yes.
          if score > best_score + 0.2:
             # if too bad, revert to best to avoid wasting
             f = best_f.copy(); A2,c_f,score = fft_self(f,N)
          w_f=log_weights_from_sequence(f); F=...; FR=...
          b=f.copy(); b_best=f.copy(); b_bound=score/(2*n) if finite else 0; b_avg=f.copy(); w_b=log_weights_from_sequence(b)
          stall=0; beta=10.0; top_frac=0.10; lr_cross=0.8; lr_self=0.5
    # final exact validation
    if n <= MAX_N:
       # validate best_f
       if exact_time < exact_limit * 2.0:
          t0e=... sc=exact_score_array(best_f)
          ...
       for item in top_list:
          sc_top=item[0]; a_top=item[1]
          if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
          if exact_time > exact_limit * 1.8: break
          if np.isfinite(exact_best_score) and sc_top > exact_best_score + 2e-3:
             continue
          t0e=... sc=exact_score_array(a_top)
          ...
    if exact_best_score < INF:
       return exact_best, float(exact_best_score), best_f, float(best_score)
    else:
       return best_f, INF, best_f, float(best_score)

Actually if w all -1e300, finite_mask true, e zeros then finite_mask true set 1 => uniform. Good. If w all set -inf? We replace nonfinite with -1e300, so finite. Good.

`recenter_w`: if w has max finite but all -1e300, m=-1e300, subtract gives zeros? Actually w - m could be nan? -1e300 - (-1e300) maybe 0 but could overflow? Same magnitude, okay. If max not finite, return zeros.

`make_weights`: For c all zeros, L=0 returns uniform. For c with one positive, top_frac maybe threshold 0.9L positive only max; mask count 1, less than4, threshold 0, mask c>0 count1, e sum exp(0)=1. Good.

`fft_self`: Use `A` variable but not used outside; return A. If c has negative small, clamp. If `max` returns negative? filled no. Good.

`exact_score_array`: For a large n, np.convolve returns length 2n-1. Good.

`generate_lengths`: Could include p*2=9612, 10240. If p small, 4096 etc. Dist sort by distance. Good. If parent size 4806, order: 4806, 4096 (710), 7209 (2403), 512? no, 8192 (3386), 9612 (4806), 2403 (2403) maybe before 8192. Actually sorted by abs: 4806, 4096, 2403 or7209 both 2403, order by n then? dist tuple (abs, v, idx), so for tie 2403 before 4096? Wait abs 710 for 4096; tie 2403: v=2403 before7209. So order: 4806,4096,2403,7209,8192,9612,10240. Quick max_len_count 5 => 4806,4096,2403,7209,8192. Good.

`propose_candidate`: If entries empty, create n. Use `parent.size` but parent may None. `n=4096 if parent is None else int(min(parent.size, MAX_N))`.

Sort entries: `s_sort=e[0] if finite else e[3]`; if s_sort not finite set 10. But if e[0] INF and e[3] INF, set 10. Good.

Build starts: In normalize loop, use for idx etc. Need not sort. When adding sinusoids, `phase = 2*np.pi*j/3 + rng.uniform(-0.05,0.05)` but default_rng uniform accepts low/high. RandomState too. Good.

In `local_search`, after f resize to MAX_N, if n changed, `extra_starts` length mismatch handled. But `budget` maybe negative if budget_s small. We handle after return.

In local_search, after setting f in symmetry break, need update best_f? We set best_score initial to old uniform score. If best_pert_score lower, update score and if score<best_score (equal old? Actually best_score initialized old, best_pert_score < score? score variable also old. We should set `score=best_pert_score` and `if score < best_score: best_score=score; best_f=f.copy()`. But best_score currently old uniform score, best_f uniform. Good. Then recompute F.

In local_search, top_list initial uniform. If symmetry break improves, update top. Good.

In while, after self line, if f changed, `score` from FFT. But `c_f` best_cc may be an array from line search; good. For safety, if `c_f` not shape (2n-1) due t? It is. If cc from tval=1 is view c_dir, okay. If later c_f modified? c_dir separate. Good.

In line search dirs, if `b_dir` equals f and t=0.5, cc=0.25c_f+0.5h_db+0.25c_dir; if b_dir=f, h_dir=c_f, c_dir=c_f => cc=c_f, no improve. Good.

In self step, if `lam_s` from c_f, but c_f may contain very small negative clamped to 0; good. If c_f all zeros (score 0 impossible), gm=0.

Now, exact validation in while: It uses `best_score < exact_best_score - 1e-5`. `exact_best_score` may INF; okay. If exact_best_score is 1.526 and best_score 1.525 (FFT), need validation; `1.525 < 1.526 -1e-5` true. Good.

Potential issue: `last_best_time` updated only when `score < best_score`. If f accepted but not global (local improvement) no update; stall? f accepted sets stall=0 anyway. `last_best_time` for restart uses global best; maybe too static but okay.

Now, if local_search run alloc 300s and best exact not updated due exact time maybe initial only. Fine.

One more thought: The exact evaluator clamps each value with `min(1000.0,x)`. Our final normalized sum=1 so max <=1 for n=1, <=? nonnegative sum1, max<=1, no cap. Good.
