```python
"""
Search for nonnegative coefficient sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a)**2)

using:
  - multi-length initial candidates from a parent sequence and Fourier/window ansatces,
  - fast FFT-based self-convolution evaluation,
  - an alternating multiplicative-weights step for the convex subproblem
    min_b max(f*b),
  - direct log-space gradient/multiplicative steps on the self-convolution,
  - O(n) line searches over convex combinations using cached convolution arrays,
  - throttled exact np.convolve validation.

The final sequence is returned normalized to sum 1, which is valid for the evaluator.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

EPS = 1e-300
INF = float("inf")
MAX_N = 10000


def next_fast_len(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_nonneg(v):
    """Return a finite nonnegative vector normalized to sum 1."""
    arr = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        arr = np.ones(max(1, arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return arr / max(s, 1.0 if s <= 0.0 else s)


def log_weights_from_sequence(a):
    """Log probabilities, centered for numerical stability."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, EPS)
    w = np.log(a)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(w))
    if np.isfinite(m):
        w = w - m
    else:
        w = np.zeros(w.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return w


def softmax_from_w(w):
    """Stable softmax of centered log weights."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, -1e300)
    m = float(np.max(w))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.ones(w.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(w.size)
    e = np.exp(w - m)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        finite = np.isfinite(w)
        if np.any(finite):
            e = np.zeros(w.size, dtype=np.float64)
            e[finite] = 1.0
            s = float(np.count_nonzero(finite))
        else:
            e = np.ones(w.size, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(w.size)
    return e / s


def recenter_w(w):
    """Center log weights and sanitize."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(w))
    if np.isfinite(m):
        w = w - m
    else:
        w = np.zeros(w.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return w


def make_weights(c, beta, top_frac=0.0):
    """
    Probabilistic weights emphasizing near-max coordinates.
    top_frac > 0 keeps only coordinates at least (1 - top_frac) * max.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    L = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(L) or L <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)

    x = (c - L) / L
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, -1e300)
    y = np.clip(float(beta) * x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)

    if top_frac > 0.0:
        thresh = L * (1.0 - float(top_frac))
        mask = c >= thresh
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt < 4:
            mask = c > 0.0
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if cnt < 1:
            mask = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
            mask[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
        e = np.where(mask, e, 0.0)

    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(m)
    return e / s


def fft_score_only(a, N):
    """FFT-based objective value for a normalized nonnegative sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size:
        if float(np.min(c)) < 0.0:
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mx = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return INF
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def fft_self(a, N):
    """Return DFT, clipped self-convolution, and score."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size:
        if float(np.min(c)) < 0.0:
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mx = float(np.max(c))
    else:
        mx = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        score = INF
    else:
        score = float(2.0 * n * mx)
    return A, c, float(score)


def grad_from_weights(lam, FR, N, n):
    """
    For fixed f, compute H^T lam where H_{x,i}=f_{x-i}.
    FR must be phase * conj(FFT(f, N)).
    """
    P = np.fft.rfft(lam, N)
    g = np.fft.irfft(P * FR, N)
    g = g[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return g


def exact_score_array(a):
    """Exact O(n^2) score using numpy.convolve."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return INF
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return INF
    b = np.convolve(a, a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return INF
    mx = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return INF
    val = float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else INF


def add_top_candidate(top, a, score, max_items=16):
    """Keep a small list of best FFT-score candidates."""
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return top
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if not top:
        top.append((float(score), a.copy()))
        return top
    worst_idx = 0
    worst_score = float(top[0][0])
    for i in range(1, len(top)):
        si = float(top[i][0])
        if si > worst_score:
            worst_score = si
            worst_idx = i
    if len(top) < max_items or score < worst_score:
        if len(top) < max_items:
            top.append((float(score), a.copy()))
        else:
            top[worst_idx] = (float(score), a.copy())
    return top


def resize_array(a, new_n):
    """Linearly resize a nonnegative sequence to a new length, then normalize."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    old_n = int(a.size)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if old_n == 1:
        arr = np.full(new_n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        return normalize_nonneg(arr)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    arr = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_nonneg(arr)


def load_initial_sequence():
    """Load the provided previous SOTA sequence if available."""
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size < 16:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    if arr.size > MAX_N:
        arr = resize_array(arr, 8192)
    return arr


def make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=2, deterministic=False, phase=0.0):
    """
    Generate a nonnegative candidate from a few cosine modes.
    For deterministic=True and K=1, this is 1 + cos(2*pi*i/n + phase).
    """
    n = int(max(1, n))
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ang = 2.0 * np.pi * idx / float(n)
    v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if K >= 1:
        if deterministic:
            a1 = 1.0
            p1 = float(phase)
        else:
            a1 = 0.4 + 1.2 * float(rng.random())
            p1 = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        v += a1 * np.cos(ang + p1)

    if K >= 2:
        if deterministic:
            a2 = 0.25
            p2 = 1.1
        else:
            a2 = 0.1 + 0.4 * float(rng.random())
            p2 = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        v += a2 * np.cos(2.0 * ang + p2)

    if K >= 3:
        if deterministic:
            a3 = 0.1
            p3 = 2.3
        else:
            a3 = 0.05 + 0.15 * float(rng.random())
            p3 = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        v += a3 * np.cos(3.0 * ang + p3)

    if K >= 4:
        if deterministic:
            a4 = 0.04
            p4 = 3.7
        else:
            a4 = 0.03 + 0.07 * float(rng.random())
            p4 = 2.0 * np.pi * float(rng.random())
        v += a4 * np.cos(4.0 * ang + p4)

    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    return normalize_nonneg(v)


def quick_best_for_length(n, parent, rng, num_random=12):
    """Quickly find a good initial candidate of fixed length n."""
    n = int(max(16, n))
    best_a = None
    best_sc = INF
    Nq = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)

    if parent is not None:
        a = resize_array(parent, n)
        sc = fft_score_only(a, Nq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a

    for j in range(8):
        a = make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=1, deterministic=True, phase=2.0 * np.pi * j / 8.0)
        sc = fft_score_only(a, Nq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a

    for _ in range(max(1, int(num_random))):
        K = 1 + int(rng.random() * 4)
        a = make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=K)
        sc = fft_score_only(a, Nq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a

    if parent is not None:
        pp = resize_array(parent, n)
        w = log_weights_from_sequence(pp) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.2, n)
        a = softmax_from_w(w)
        sc = fft_score_only(a, Nq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a

        w = log_weights_from_sequence(pp) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, n)
        a = softmax_from_w(w)
        sc = fft_score_only(a, Nq)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_sc = 2.0
    return best_a, float(best_sc)


def generate_lengths(parent):
    """Choose a few promising sequence lengths, preferring the parent length."""
    raw = []
    if parent is not None:
        p = int(parent.size)
        vals = [p, max(1024, p // 2), int(p * 1.5), p * 2, 2048, 4096, 8192, 10240]
        for v in vals:
            if 512 <= int(v) <= MAX_N:
                raw.append(int(v))
    else:
        raw = [2048, 4096, 8192]

    seen = set()
    uniq = []
    for v in raw:
        if v not in seen:
            seen.add(v)
            uniq.append(v)

    if parent is not None:
        p = int(parent.size)
        dist = []
        for i, v in enumerate(uniq):
            dist.append((abs(v - p), v, i))
    else:
        dist = []
        for i, v in enumerate(uniq):
            dist.append((v, v, i))
    dist.sort()

    out = []
    for _, _, i in dist:
        out.append(uniq[i])
    return out


def local_search(a_start, extra_starts, budget_s, rng):
    """
    Local optimizer for a fixed starting length.
    Returns (exact_best_array, exact_best_score, fft_best_array, fft_best_score).
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)

    f = normalize_nonneg(a_start)
    n = int(f.size)
    if n < 512:
        f = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        n = 1024
    if n > MAX_N:
        f = resize_array(f, MAX_N)
        n = int(f.size)

    N2quick = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    if budget <= 1.0:
        sc = fft_score_only(f, N2quick)
        exc = exact_score_array(f)
        return f, exc, f, sc

    deadline = start + budget
    N = next_fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    phase = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * np.arange(N // 2 + 1) * (n - 1) / float(N))

    F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
    FR = phase * np.conjugate(F)
    A2, c_f, score = fft_self(f, N)

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_score = float(score)
    top_list = add_top_candidate([], best_f, best_score)

    b = f.copy()
    b_best = f.copy()
    b_bound = (score / (2.0 * n)) if np.isfinite(score) else 0.0
    b_avg = f.copy()

    w_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
    w_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)

    beta = 10.0
    top_frac = 0.10
    lr_cross = 0.8
    lr_self = 0.5

    stall = 0
    outer = 0
    last_best_time = start

    exact_best = f.copy()
    exact_best_score = INF
    exact_time = 0.0
    exact_calls = 0
    last_exact = start
    exact_limit = max(5.0, 0.10 * budget)

    # Initial exact validation.
    if exact_limit > 0.0:
        t0e = time.monotonic()
        sc = exact_score_array(f)
        exact_time += time.monotonic() - t0e
        exact_calls += 1
        last_exact = time.monotonic()
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < exact_best_score:
            exact_best_score = float(sc)
            exact_best = f.copy()

    # If the start is nearly uniform, break symmetry immediately.
    if np.isfinite(score) and score > 1.9:
        best_pert = f.copy()
        best_pert_score = float(score)
        best_pert_c = c_f.copy()
        for _ in range(30):
            cand = make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=1 + int(rng.random() * 3))
            A_try, c_try, sc_try = fft_self(cand, N)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_pert_score:
                best_pert_score = float(sc_try)
                best_pert = cand.copy()
                best_pert_c = c_try.copy()
        if best_pert_score < score - 1e-12:
            f = best_pert.copy()
            score = best_pert_score
            c_f = best_pert_c
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_f = f.copy()
                top_list = add_top_candidate(top_list, best_f, best_score)
            w_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
            F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
            FR = phase * np.conjugate(F)
            b = f.copy()
            b_best = f.copy()
            b_bound = score / (2.0 * n)
            b_avg = f.copy()
            w_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        outer += 1

        # Safety reset if numeric state becomes invalid.
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            f = best_f.copy()
            F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
            FR = phase * np.conjugate(F)
            A2, c_f, score = fft_self(f, N)
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
                F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
                FR = phase * np.conjugate(F)
                A2, c_f, score = fft_self(f, N)
            w_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
            b = f.copy()
            b_best = f.copy()
            b_bound = score / (2.0 * n) if np.isfinite(score) else 0.0
            b_avg = f.copy()
            w_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)
            stall = 0
            continue

        # Periodic exact validation of the current FFT best.
        if n <= MAX_N and exact_time < exact_limit:
            need = (exact_calls == 0 or
                    time.monotonic() - last_exact > 30.0 or
                    best_score < exact_best_score - 1e-5)
            if need:
                t0e = time.monotonic()
                sc = exact_score_array(best_f)
                exact_time += time.monotonic() - t0e
                exact_calls += 1
                last_exact = time.monotonic()
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < exact_best_score:
                    exact_best_score = float(sc)
                    exact_best = best_f.copy()

        # --- Alternating convex subproblem: optimize b against fixed f ---
        cross_iters = 3
        if outer % 5 == 0:
            cross_iters = 6

        for _ in range(cross_iters):
            B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
            h = np.fft.irfft(F * B, N)
            h = h[:2 * n - 1]
            if h.size:
                if float(np.min(h)) < 0.0:
                    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
                hmax = float(np.max(h))
            else:
                hmax = 0.0

            if np.isfinite(hmax) and hmax < b_bound:
                b_bound = hmax
                b_best = b.copy()

            b_avg = 0.9 * b_avg + 0.1 * b
            s_avg = float(np.sum(b_avg))
            if np.isfinite(s_avg) and s_avg > 0.0:
                b_avg = b_avg / s_avg
            else:
                b_avg = b.copy()

            lam = make_weights(h, beta, top_frac)
            g = grad_from_weights(lam, FR, N, n)
            gm = float(np.max(g))
            if np.isfinite(gm) and gm > 0.0:
                w_b = w_b + lr_cross * (-g / gm)
                w_b = recenter_w(w_b)
                b_new = softmax_from_w(w_b)
                s_new = float(np.sum(b_new))
                if np.isfinite(s_new) and s_new > 0.0:
                    b = b_new
                else:
                    b = best_f.copy()
                    w_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)

        # --- Line search over convex combinations f and candidate b ---
        best_t = 0.0
        best_mx = float(score)
        best_cc = c_f
        best_b = None

        # Evaluate current best b and most recent b.
        for dir_index in range(2):
            if dir_index == 0:
                b_dir = b_best
            else:
                b_dir = b

            if b_dir.size != n:
                continue
            if not bool(np.all(np.isfinite(b_dir))):
                continue

            B_dir = np.fft.rfft(b_dir, N)
            h_dir = np.fft.irfft(F * B_dir, N)
            h_dir = h_dir[:2 * n - 1]
            if h_dir.size and float(np.min(h_dir)) < 0.0:
                h_dir = np.maximum(h_dir, 0.0)

            c_dir = np.fft.irfft(B_dir * B_dir, N)
            c_dir = c_dir[:2 * n - 1]
            if float(np.min(c_dir)) < 0.0:
                c_dir = np.maximum(c_dir, 0.0)

            for tval in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
                om = 1.0 - tval
                if tval == 0.0:
                    cc = c_f
                elif tval == 1.0:
                    cc = c_dir
                else:
                    cc = (om * om) * c_f + (2.0 * tval * om) * h_dir + (tval * tval) * c_dir

                if float(np.min(cc)) < 0.0:
                    cc = np.maximum(cc, 0.0)
                mx = float(np.max(cc))
                sc_t = 2.0 * n * mx
                if np.isfinite(sc_t) and sc_t < best_mx:
                    best_mx = float(sc_t)
                    best_t = tval
                    best_cc = cc
                    best_b = b_dir.copy()

        if best_b is not None and best_t > 0.0 and best_mx < score - 1e-12:
            f = (1.0 - best_t) * f + best_t * best_b
            sf = float(np.sum(f))
            if np.isfinite(sf) and sf > 0.0:
                f = f / sf
            else:
                f = best_f.copy()
                A2, c_f, score = fft_self(f, N)

            if np.isfinite(best_mx):
                score = best_mx
                c_f = best_cc
            else:
                A2, c_f, score = fft_self(f, N)

            if score < best_score:
                best_score = float(score)
                best_f = f.copy()
                top_list = add_top_candidate(top_list, best_f, best_score)
                last_best_time = time.monotonic()

            w_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
            F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
            FR = phase * np.conjugate(F)

            if best_t >= 0.9:
                b = f.copy()
                b_best = f.copy()
                b_bound = score / (2.0 * n) if np.isfinite(score) else 0.0
                b_avg = f.copy()
                w_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)
            else:
                b = best_b.copy()
                b_best = best_b.copy()
                b_bound = 10.0
                b_avg = best_b.copy()
                w_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)

            stall = 0
            lr_cross = min(1.6, lr_cross * 1.05)
            lr_self = min(1.4, lr_self * 1.05)
            beta = min(50.0, beta * 1.01 + 0.05)
        else:
            stall += 1
            lr_cross = max(0.25, lr_cross * 0.97)

        # --- Direct self-convolution step ---
        if outer % 2 == 0 or stall > 8:
            lam_s = make_weights(c_f, beta, 0.05)
            g_s = grad_from_weights(lam_s, FR, N, n)
            gm = float(np.max(g_s))
            if np.isfinite(gm) and gm > 0.0:
                best_sc = float(score)
                best_f_try = None
                best_w_try = None
                best_c_try = None

                for lr in (lr_self, 0.5 * lr_self, 2.0 * lr_self):
                    w_try = w_f - lr * (g_s / gm)
                    w_try = recenter_w(w_try)
                    f_try = softmax_from_w(w_try)
                    A_try = np.fft.rfft(f_try, N)
                    c_try = np.fft.irfft(A_try * A_try, N)
                    c_try = c_try[:2 * n - 1]
                    if float(np.min(c_try)) < 0.0:
                        c_try = np.maximum(c_try, 0.0)
                    mx_try = float(np.max(c_try))
                    sc_try = 2.0 * n * mx_try
                    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc:
                        best_sc = float(sc_try)
                        best_f_try = f_try
                        best_w_try = w_try
                        best_c_try = c_try

                if best_f_try is not None:
                    f = best_f_try
                    w_f = best_w_try
                    c_f = best_c_try
                    score = best_sc
                    F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
                    FR = phase * np.conjugate(F)

                    if score < best_score:
                        best_score = float(score)
                        best_f = f.copy()
                        top_list = add_top_candidate(top_list, best_f, best_score)
                        last_best_time = time.monotonic()

                    b = f.copy()
                    b_best = f.copy()
                    b_bound = score / (2.0 * n) if np.isfinite(score) else 0.0
                    b_avg = f.copy()
                    w_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)

                    stall = 0
                    lr_self = min(1.4, lr_self * 1.05)
                    beta = min(50.0, beta * 1.01 + 0.05)
                else:
                    stall += 1
                    lr_self = max(0.2, lr_self * 0.95)

        # Slow schedule.
        if outer % 50 == 0:
            beta = min(50.0, beta * 1.05)
            top_frac = max(0.04, min(0.18, top_frac * 1.1))

        # Restart / exploration.
        if stall > 120 or (time.monotonic() - last_best_time > max(120.0, 0.2 * budget)):
            chosen = None
            if extra_starts is not None:
                for arr in extra_starts:
                    if arr is not None and arr.size == n:
                        chosen = arr
                        break

            if chosen is not None:
                f = normalize_nonneg(chosen)
            else:
                sigma = 0.1 + 0.2 * float(rng.random())
                w_try = log_weights_from_sequence(best_f) + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
                f = softmax_from_w(w_try)

            A2, c_f, score = fft_self(f, N)
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                f = best_f.copy()
                A2, c_f, score = fft_self(f, N)

            if score > best_score + 0.2:
                f = best_f.copy()
                A2, c_f, score = fft_self(f, N)

            if score < best_score:
                best_score = float(score)
                best_f = f.copy()
                top_list = add_top_candidate(top_list, best_f, best_score)

            w_f = log_weights_from_sequence(f)
            F = np.fft.rfft(f, N)
            FR = phase * np.conjugate(F)
            b = f.copy()
            b_best = f.copy()
            b_bound = score / (2.0 * n) if np.isfinite(score) else 0.0
            b_avg = f.copy()
            w_b = log_weights_from_sequence(b)

            stall = 0
            beta = 10.0
            top_frac = 0.10
            lr_cross = 0.8
            lr_self = 0.5

    # Final exact checks for retained FFT-best candidates.
    if n <= MAX_N:
        if exact_time < exact_limit * 2.0:
            t0e = time.monotonic()
            sc = exact_score_array(best_f)
            exact_time += time.monotonic() - t0e
            exact_calls += 1
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < exact_best_score:
                exact_best_score = float(sc)
                exact_best = best_f.copy()

        for item in top_list:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            if exact_time > exact_limit * 1.8:
                break
            sc_top = float(item[0])
            a_top = item[1]
            if np.isfinite(exact_best_score) and sc_top > exact_best_score + 2e-3:
                continue
            t0e = time.monotonic()
            sc = exact_score_array(a_top)
            exact_time += time.monotonic() - t0e
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < exact_best_score:
                exact_best_score = float(sc)
                exact_best = a_top.copy()

    if np.isfinite(exact_best_score):
        return exact_best, float(exact_best_score), best_f, float(best_score)
    return best_f, INF, best_f, float(best_score)


def finalize_sequence(a):
    """Return a valid list of nonnegative floats, normalized to sum 1."""
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)

    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
        a = a / s
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 < 0.01:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = a / s2

    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point required by the harness.

    Finds and polishes a good sequence within the time budget.
    """
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = min(budget, 2395.0)

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    parent = load_initial_sequence()

    quick_budget = min(120.0, max(20.0, 0.05 * budget))
    quick_deadline = t0 + quick_budget
    max_len_count = 5 if budget > 1800 else (4 if budget > 800 else 2)

    lengths = generate_lengths(parent)
    if len(lengths) > max_len_count:
        lengths = lengths[:max_len_count]

    entries = []
    quick_exact_count = 0
    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= quick_deadline:
            break
        if n < 256 or n > MAX_N:
            continue
        a_quick, sc_fft = quick_best_for_length(n, parent, rng, num_random=12 if n > 8192 else 16)
        a_quick = normalize_nonneg(a_quick)

        sc_exact = INF
        if quick_exact_count < 3 and a_quick.size <= MAX_N and time.monotonic() < quick_deadline - 5.0:
            t1 = time.monotonic()
            sc_exact = exact_score_array(a_quick)
            quick_exact_count += 1
            if not np.isfinite(sc_exact):
                sc_exact = sc_fft

        if not np.isfinite(sc_exact):
            sc_exact = sc_fft
        entries.append([sc_exact, n, a_quick, sc_fft])

    if not entries:
        n = 4096 if parent is None else int(min(parent.size, MAX_N))
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        entries = [[INF, n, a, INF]]

    sort_keys = []
    for idx, e in enumerate(entries):
        s_sort = e[0] if np.isfinite(e[0]) else e[3]
        if not np.isfinite(s_sort):
            s_sort = 10.0
        sort_keys.append((s_sort, e[1], idx))
    sort_keys.sort()
    ordered = [entries[k[2]] for k in sort_keys]

    total_budget = max(0.0, budget - (time.monotonic() - t0) - 10.0)
    if total_budget < 30.0:
        return finalize_sequence(ordered[0][2])

    max_runs = 3 if total_budget > 1500 else (2 if total_budget > 700 else 1)
    runs = []

    for e in ordered:
        if len(runs) >= max_runs:
            break
        sc_sort, n, a_quick, sc_fft = e

        starts = []
        starts.append(normalize_nonneg(a_quick))
        if parent is not None:
            starts.append(normalize_nonneg(resize_array(parent, n)))
        for j in range(3):
            ph = 2.0 * np.pi * j / 3.0 + rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05)
            starts.append(normalize_nonneg(make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=1, deterministic=True, phase=ph)))
        for _ in range(3):
            K = 1 + int(rng.random() * 3)
            starts.append(normalize_nonneg(make_fourier_candidate(n, rng, K=K)))

        if parent is not None:
            pp = resize_array(parent, n)
            w = log_weights_from_sequence(pp) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, n)
            starts.append(softmax_from_w(w))

        Nq = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
        best_start = starts[0]
        best_start_score = fft_score_only(best_start, Nq)
        for arr in starts[1:]:
            sc = fft_score_only(arr, Nq)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_start_score:
                best_start_score = sc
                best_start = arr

        runs.append([n, best_start, starts, sc_sort, a_quick, sc_fft])

    if not runs:
        return finalize_sequence(ordered[0][2])

    m = len(runs)
    if m == 1:
        fractions = [1.0]
    elif m == 2:
        fractions = [0.65, 0.35]
    else:
        fractions = [0.50, 0.30, 0.20]

    global_exact_arr = None
    global_exact_score = INF
    global_fft_arr = None
    global_fft_score = INF

    for run in runs:
        ex_score = run[3]
        ex_arr = run[4]
        ft_score = run[5]
        ft_arr = run[4]
        if np.isfinite(ex_score) and ex_score < global_exact_score:
            global_exact_score = ex_score
            global_exact_arr = ex_arr
        if np.isfinite(ft_score) and ft_score < global_fft_score:
            global_fft_score = ft_score
            global_fft_arr = ft_arr

    for i, run in enumerate(runs):
        n, best_start, starts, sc_sort, a_quick, sc_fft = run
        alloc = total_budget * fractions[i]
        remaining = t0 + budget - 5.0 - time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(alloc, max(10.0, remaining))
        if alloc < 15.0:
            continue

        arr_out, ex_score_out, fft_out, fft_score_out = local_search(
            best_start, starts, alloc, rng
        )

        if np.isfinite(ex_score_out) and ex_score_out < global_exact_score:
            global_exact_score = float(ex_score_out)
            global_exact_arr = arr_out
        if np.isfinite(fft_score_out) and fft_score_out < global_fft_score:
            global_fft_score = float(fft_score_out)
            global_fft_arr = fft_out

    if np.isfinite(global_exact_score):
        if (np.isfinite(global_fft_score) and
                global_fft_arr is not None and
                global_fft_score < global_exact_score - 1e-6):
            return finalize_sequence(global_fft_arr)
        if global_exact_arr is not None:
            return finalize_sequence(global_exact_arr)

    if np.isfinite(global_fft_score) and global_fft_arr is not None:
        return finalize_sequence(global_fft_arr)

    if parent is not None:
        return finalize_sequence(parent)

    return finalize_sequence(np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64))
```
